T>\nT kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n dims[3]) {\n    double best = -1.0;\n    for (int px = 1; px <= P; ++px) {\n        if (P % px) continue;\n        for (int py = 1; py <= P / px; ++py) {\n            if ((P / px) % py) continue;\n            const int pz = P / px / py;\n            if ((size_t)px > nx || (size_t)py > ny || (size_t)pz > nz) continue;\n            // Per-rank exchanged face area; x-faces are strided, so weight them a bit more.\n            const double bx = (double)nx / px, by = (double)ny / py, bz = (double)nz / pz;\n            const double cost = (px > 1 ? 1.5 * 2.0 * by * bz : 0.0)\n                              + (py > 1 ? 2.0 * bx * bz : 0.0)\n                              + (pz > 1 ? 2.0 * bx * by : 0.0);\n            if (best < 0.0 || cost < best - 1e-9) {\n                best = cost;\n                dims[0] = px; dims[1] = py; dims[2] = pz;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return best >= 0.0;\n}\n\nstatic void setupDomain(Domain& d, MPI_Comm comm, const int dims[3]) {\n    int periods[3] = {0, 0, 0};\n    int mpiDims[3] = {dims[2], dims[1], dims[0]};  // MPI order: z, y, x\n    MPI_Cart_create(comm, 3, mpiDims, periods, 1, &d.comm);\n    int rank;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(d.comm, &rank);\n    int coords[3];\n    MPI_Cart_coords(d.comm, rank, 3, coords);\n    blockRange(d.nz, dims[2], coords[0], d.oz, d.lz);\n    blockRange(d.ny, dims[1], coords[1], d.oy, d.ly);\n    blockRange(d.nx, dims[0], coords[2], d.ox, d.lx);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 2, 1, &d.nbrLo[0], &d.nbrHi[0]);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 1, 1, &d.nbrLo[1], &d.nbrHi[1]);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 0, 1, &d.nbrLo[2], &d.nbrHi[2]);\n\n    d.sx = d.lx + 2;\n    d.sy = d.sx * (d.ly + 2);\n    d.localSize = d.sy * (d.lz + 2);\n\n    const int sizes[3] = {(int)d.lz + 2, (int)d.ly + 2, (int)d.lx + 2};\n    const int l[3] = {(int)d.lx, (int)d.ly, (int)d.lz};\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        // Subarray in (z,y,x) order; MPI index = 2 - dim\n        int sub[3] = {l[2], l[1], l[0]};\n        const int m = 2 - dim;\n        sub[m] = 1;\n        int st[3] = {1, 1, 1};\n        auto make = [&](int pos, MPI_Datatype& t) {\n            st[m] = pos;\n            MPI_Type_create_subarray(3, sizes, sub, st, MPI_ORDER_C, MPI_DOUBLE, &t);\n            MPI_Type_commit(&t);\n        };\n        make(1, d.sendLo[dim]);\n        make(l[dim], d.sendHi[dim]);\n        make(0, d.recvLo[dim]);\n        make(l[dim] + 1, d.recvHi[dim]);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void freeDomain(Domain& d) {\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.sendLo[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.sendHi[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.recvLo[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.recvHi[dim]);\n    }\n    MPI_Comm_free(&d.comm);\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const Domain& d) {\n    // Initialize owned cells and in-domain halo cells from the global formula\n    for (size_t lz = 0; lz < d.lz + 2; ++lz) {\n        const long gz = (long)d.oz + (long)lz - 1;\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= (long)d.nz) continue;\n        for (size_t ly = 0; ly < d.ly + 2; ++ly) {\n            const long gy = (long)d.oy + (long)ly - 1;\n            if (gy < 0 || gy >= (long)d.ny) continue;\n            for (size_t lx = 0; lx < d.lx + 2; ++lx) {\n                const long gx = (long)d.ox + (long)lx - 1;\n                if (gx < 0 || gx >= (long)d.nx) continue;\n                const size_t idx = idx3(gx, gy, gz, d.nx, d.ny);\n                grid[d.lidx(lx, ly, lz)] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nstruct Box { size_t x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1; };  // half-open local index ranges\n\n// 7-point stencil on a local box\nstatic void stencilBox(const Real* __restrict in, Real* __restrict out, const Domain& d, const Box& b) {\n    if (b.x0 >= b.x1 || b.y0 >= b.y1 || b.z0 >= b.z1) return;\n    const size_t sx = d.sx, sy = d.sy;\n    for (size_t z = b.z0; z < b.z1; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = b.y0; y < b.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t row = z * sy + y * sx;\n            const Real* __restrict c = in + row;\n            const Real* __restrict f = c - sx;\n            const Real* __restrict k = c + sx;\n            const Real* __restrict bo = c - sy;\n            const Real* __restrict t = c + sy;\n            Real* __restrict o = out + row;\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n            for (size_t x = b.x0; x < b.x1; ++x) {\n                // Simple averaging stencil (same operation order as the reference)\n                o[x] = (c[x] + c[x - 1] + c[x + 1] + f[x] + k[x] + bo[x] + t[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Local index range [lo,hi) of globally interior cells within the owned block\nstatic void interiorRange(size_t n, size_t off, size_t len, size_t& lo, size_t& hi) {\n    const size_t g0 = std::max<size_t>(off, 1);\n    const size_t g1 = std::min<size_t>(off + len, n >= 1 ? n - 1 : 0);\n    if (g1 <= g0) { lo = hi = 1; return; }\n    lo = g0 - off + 1;\n    hi = g1 - off + 1;\n}\n\n// One stencil iteration with halo exchange overlapped with inner computation.\n// Global boundary cells are never written, so they keep their (copied) initial values.\nvoid stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output, const Domain& d, const Box& upd) {\n    MPI_Request reqs[12];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    Real* inp = input.data();\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Irecv(inp, 1, d.recvLo[dim], d.nbrLo[dim], 2 * dim, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Irecv(inp, 1, d.recvHi[dim], d.nbrHi[dim], 2 * dim + 1, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Isend(inp, 1, d.sendHi[dim], d.nbrHi[dim], 2 * dim, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(inp, 1, d.sendLo[dim], d.nbrLo[dim], 2 * dim + 1, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n\n    // Inner box: cells that do not depend on neighbor halos\n    Box in = upd;\n    if (d.nbrLo[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x0 = std::max<size_t>(in.x0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x1 = std::min<size_t>(in.x1, d.lx);\n    if (d.nbrLo[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y0 = std::max<size_t>(in.y0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y1 = std::min<size_t>(in.y1, d.ly);\n    if (d.nbrLo[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z0 = std::max<size_t>(in.z0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z1 = std::min<size_t>(in.z1, d.lz);\n    const bool innerValid = in.x0 < in.x1 && in.y0 < in.y1 && in.z0 < in.z1;\n\n    Real* out = output.data();\n    if (innerValid) stencilBox(inp, out, d, in);\n\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!innerValid) {\n        stencilBox(inp, out, d, upd);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Shell = upd \\ in\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, upd.z0, in.z0});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, in.z1, upd.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, in.y0, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, in.y1, upd.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, in.x0, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {in.x1, upd.x1, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n}\n\n// Gather the distributed grid into a global array on rank 0 of the given communicator\nstatic void gatherGrid(const std::vector<Real>& local, const Domain& d, std::vector<Real>& global) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(d.comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(d.comm, &size);\n    std::vector<Real> packed(d.lx * d.ly * d.lz);\n    size_t p = 0;\n    for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lz; ++z)\n        for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.ly; ++y) {\n            const Real* src = local.data() + d.lidx(1, y, z);\n            std::copy(src, src + d.lx, packed.data() + p);\n            p += d.lx;\n        }\n    unsigned long long meta[6] = {d.ox, d.oy, d.oz, d.lx, d.ly, d.lz};\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> allMeta(rank == 0 ? 6 * size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(meta, 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, allMeta.data(), 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, d.comm);\n\n    std::vector<int> counts, displs;\n    std::vector<Real> recv;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        counts.resize(size);\n        displs.resize(size);\n        size_t total = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            counts[r] = (int)(allMeta[6 * r + 3] * allMeta[6 * r + 4] * allMeta[6 * r + 5]);\n            displs[r] = (int)total;\n            total += counts[r];\n        }\n        recv.resize(total);\n    }\n    MPI_Gatherv(packed.data(), (int)packed.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, recv.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, d.comm);\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            const unsigned long long* m = &allMeta[6 * r];\n            const Real* src = recv.data() + displs[r];\n            for (size_t z = 0; z < m[5]; ++z)\n                for (size_t y = 0; y < m[4]; ++y) {\n                    std::copy(src, src + m[3], global.data() + idx3(m[0], m[1] + y, m[2] + z, d.nx, d.ny));\n                    src += m[3];\n                }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_mid+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <mpi.h>\\n',1)\n\nmstart=src.index('int main(')\nmain=r'''int main(int argc, validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n       validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root)      if (lead) {\n                // Print results for external validation\n                if (printResults) {\n                    print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n                }\n\n d.recvHi[dim]);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void freeDomain(Domain& d) {\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.sendLo[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.sendHi[dim]);\n owned cells and in-domain halo cells from the global formula\n    for (size_t lz = 0; lz < d.lz + 2; ++lz) {\n        const long gz = (long)d.oz + (long)lz - 1;\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= (long)d.nz) continue;\n        for (size_t ly = 0; ly < d.ly + 2; ++ly) {\n            const long gy = (long)d.oy + (long)ly - 1;\n            if (gy < 0 || gy >= (long)d.ny) continue;\n index ranges\n\n// 7-point stencil on a local box\nstatic void stencilBox(const Real* __restrict in, Real* __restrict out, const Domain& d, const Box& b) {\n    if (b.x0 >= b.x1 || b.y0 >= b.y1 || b.z0 >= b.z1) return;\n    const size_t sx = d.sx, sy = d.sy;\n    for (size_t z = b.z0; z < b.z1; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = b.y0; y < b.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t row = z * sy + y * sx;\n            const Real* __restrict c = in + row;\n            const Real* __restrict f = c - sx;\n            const Real* __restrict k = c + sx;\n            const Real* __restrict bo = c - sy;\n            const Real* __restrict t = c + sy;\n            Real* __restrict o = out + row;\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n            for (size_t x = b.x0; x < b.x1; ++x) {\n                // Simple averaging stencil (same operation order as the reference)\n                o[x] = (c[x] + c[x - 1] + c[x + 1] + f[x] + k[x] + bo[x] + t[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Local index range [lo,hi) of globally interior cells within the owned block\nstatic void interiorRange(size_t n, size_t off, size_t len, size_t& lo, size_t& hi) {\n    const size_t g0 = std::max<size_t>(off, 1);\n    const size_t g1 = std::min<size_t>(off + len, n >= 1 ? n - 1 : 0);\n    if (g1 <= g0) { lo = hi = 1; return; }\n    lo = g0 - off + 1;\n    hi = g1 - off + 1;\n}\n\n// One stencil iteration with halo exchange overlapped with inner computation.\n// Global boundary cells are never written, so they keep their (copied) initial values.\nvoid stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output, const Domain& d, const Box& upd) {\n    MPI_Request reqs[12];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    Real* inp = input.data();\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Irecv(inp, 1, d.recvLo[dim], d.nbrLo[dim], 2 * dim, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Irecv(inp, 1, d.recvHi[dim], d.nbrHi[dim], 2 * dim + 1, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Isend(inp, 1, d.sendHi[dim], d.nbrHi[dim], 2 * dim, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(inp, 1, d.sendLo[dim], d.nbrLo[dim], 2 * dim + 1, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n\n    // Inner box: cells that do not depend on neighbor halos\n    Box in = upd;\n    if (d.nbrLo[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x0 = std::max<size_t>(in.x0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x1 = std::min<size_t>(in.x1, d.lx);\n    if (d.nbrLo[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y0 = std::max<size_t>(in.y0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y1 = std::min<size_t>(in.y1, d.ly);\n    if (d.nbrLo[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z0 = std::max<size_t>(in.z0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z1 = std::min<size_t>(in.z1, d.lz);\n    const bool innerValid = in.x0 < in.x1 && in.y0 < in.y1 && in.z0 < in.z1;\n\n    Real* out = output.data();\n    if (innerValid) stencilBox(inp, out, d, in);\n\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!innerValid) {\n        stencilBox(inp, out, d, upd);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Shell = upd \\ in\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, upd.z0, in.z0});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, in.z1, upd.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, in.y0, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, in.y1, upd.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, in.x0, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {in.x1, upd.x1, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n}\n\n// Gather the distributed grid into a global array on rank 0 of the given communicator\nstatic void gatherGrid(const std::vector<Real>& local, const Domain& d, std::vector<Real>& global) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(d.comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(d.comm, &size);\n    std::vector<Real> packed(d.lx * d.ly * d.lz);\n    size_t p = 0;\n    for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lz; ++z)\n        for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.ly; ++y) {\n            const Real* src = local.data() + d.lidx(1, y, z);\n            std::copy(src, src + d.lx, packed.data() + p);\n            p += d.lx;\n        }\n    unsigned long long meta[6] = {d.ox, d.oy, d.oz, d.lx, d.ly, d.lz};\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> allMeta(rank == 0 ? 6 * size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(meta, 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, allMeta.data(), 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, d.comm);\n\n    std::vector<int> counts, displs;\n    std::vector<Real> recv;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        counts.resize(size);\n        displs.resize(size);\n        size_t total = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            counts[r] = (int)(allMeta[6 * r + 3] * allMeta[6 * r + 4] * allMeta[6 * r + 5]);\n            displs[r] = (int)total;\n            total += counts[r];\n        }\n        recv.resize(total);\n    }\n    MPI_Gatherv(packed.data(), (int)packed.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, recv.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, d.comm);\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            const unsigned long long* m = &allMeta[6 * r];\n            const Real* src = recv.data() + displs[r];\n            for (size_t z = 0; z < m[5]; ++z)\n                for (size_t y = 0; y < m[4]; ++y) {\n                    std::copy(src, src + m[3], global.data() + idx3(m[0], m[1] + y, m[2] + z, d.nx, d.ny));\n                    src += m[3];\n                }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_mid+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include block decomposition).\n// The local array stores the owned block plus a one-cell halo on every side.\nstruct Domain {\n    size_t nx, ny, nz;              // global sizes\n    size_t lx, ly, lz;              // owned block sizes\n    size_t ox, oy, oz;              // global offset of owned block\n    size_t sx, sy;                  // local array strides (lx+2, (lx+2)*(ly+2))\n    size_t lidx(size_t x, size_t y, size_t z) const noexcept { return z * sy + y * sx + x; }\n};\n\nstatic void blockRange(size_t n, int p, int c, size_t& off, size_t& len) {\n    const size_t base = n / p, rem = n % p;\n    off = c * base + std::min<size_t>(c, rem);\n    len = base + (static_cast<size_t>(c) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// Choose a process grid (px,py,pz) with px*py*pz == P that minimizes halo traffic.\n// Returns false if no factorization gives every rank at least one cell per dimension.\nstatic bool chooseDims(int P, size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, int dims[3]) {\n    double best = -1.0;\n    for (int px = 1; px <= P; ++px) {\n        if (P % px) continue;\n        for (int py = 1; py <= P / px; ++py) {\n            if ((P / px) % py) continue;\n            const int pz = P / px / py;\n            if ((size_t)px > nx || (size_t)py > ny || (size_t)pz > nz) continue;\n            // Per-rank exchanged face area; x-faces are strided, so weight them a bit more.\n            const double bx = (double)nx / px, by = (double)ny / py, bz = (double)nz / pz;\n            const double cost = (px > 1 ? 1.5 * 2.0 * by * bz : 0.0)\n                              + (py > 1 ? 2.0 * bx * bz : 0.0)\n                              + (pz > 1 ? 2.0 * bx * by : 0.0);\n            if (best < 0.0 || cost < best - 1e-9) {\n                best = cost;\n                dims[0] = px; dims[1] = py; Inner box: cells that do not depend on neighbor halos\n    Box in = upd;\n    if (d.nbrLo[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x0 = std::max<size_t>(in.x0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x1 = std::min<size_t>(in.x1, d.lx);\n    if (d.nbrLo[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y0 = std::max<size_t>(in.y0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y1 = std::min<size_t>(in.y1, d.ly);\n    if (d.nbrLo[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z0 = std::max<size_t>(in.z0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z1 = std::min<size_t>(in.z1, d.lz);\n    const bool innerValid = in.x0 < in.x1 && in.y0 < in.y1 && in.z0 < in.z1;\n\n    Real* out = output.data();\n    if (innerValid) stencilBox(inp, out, d, in);\n\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!innerValid) {\n        stencilBox(inp, out, d, upd);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Shell = upd \\ in\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, upd.z0, in.z0});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, in.z1, upd.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, in.y0, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, in.y1, upd.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, in.x0, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {in.x1, upd.x1, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n}\n\n// Gather the distributed grid into a global array on rank 0 of the given communicator\nstatic void gatherGrid(const std::vector<Real>& local, const Domain& d, std::vector<Real>& global) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(d.comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(d.comm, &size);\n    std::vector<Real> packed(d.lx * d.ly * d.lz);\n    size_t p = 0;\n    for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lz; ++z)\n        for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.ly; ++y) {\n            const Real* src = local.data() + d.lidx(1, y, z);\n            std::copy(src, src + d.lx, packed.data() + p);\n            p += d.lx;\n        }\n    unsigned long long meta[6] = {d.ox, d.oy, d.oz, d.lx, d.ly, d.lz};\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> allMeta(rank == 0 ? 6 * size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(meta, 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, allMeta.data(), 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, d.comm);\n\n    std::vector<int> counts, displs;\n    std::vector<Real> recv;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        counts.resize(size);\n        displs.resize(size);\n        size_t total = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            counts[r] = (int)(allMeta[6 * r + 3] * allMeta[6 * r + 4] * allMeta[6 * r + 5]);\n            displs[r] = (int)total;\n            total += counts[r];\n        }\n        recv.resize(total);\n    }\n    MPI_Gatherv(packed.data(), (int)packed.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, recv.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, d.comm);\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            const unsigned long long* m = &allMeta[6 * r];\n            const Real* src = recv.data() + displs[r];\n            for (size_t z = 0; z < m[5]; ++z)\n                for (size_t y = 0; y < m[4]; ++y) {\n                    std::copy(src, src + m[3], global.data() + idx3(m[0], m[1] + y, m[2] + z, d.nx, d.ny));\n                    src += m[3];\n                }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_mid+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <mpi.h>\\n',1)\n\nmstart=src.index('int main(')\nmain=r'''int main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int worldRank, worldSize;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);\n    const bool root = (worldRank == 0);\n\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n    \n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Pick the largest number of active ranks that admits a non-empty block decomposition\n    int dims[3] = {1, 1, 1};\n    int active = worldSize;\n    while (active > 1 && !chooseDims(active, nx, ny, nz, dims)) --active;\n    if (active == 1) { dims[0] = dims[1] = dims[2] = 1; }\n    MPI_Comm activeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, worldRank < active ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED, worldRank, &activeComm);\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if   const bool lead = (rank == 0);\n\n        // Allocate local grids with halos (double buffering)\n        std::vector<Real> grid1(d.localSize, 0.0);\n        std::vector<Real> grid2(d.localSize, 0.0);\n\n        // Initialize\n        if (root) printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n        initializeGrid(grid1, d);\n        grid2 = grid1;  // boundary values are copied unchanged every (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n            double mcups = cellUpdates / (durationMs / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n            printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n        }\n\n        if (printResults || validate) {\n            const std::vector<Real>& localFinal = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n            std::vector<Real> finalGrid(lead ? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfiva2UfwFX2CSGwm7Ped","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_016qZWPnimCfgn5hba91J5NX","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261002-142720/stencil3d_claude-opus-5.5-cc-medium_mpi_r4/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Local subdomain of the global grid owned by one MPI rank (3D block decomposition).\n// The local array stores the owned block plus a one-cell halo on every side.\nstruct Domain {\n    size_t nx, ny, nz;              // global sizes\n    size_t lx, ly, lz;              // owned block sizes\n    size_t ox, oy, oz;              // global offset of owned block\n    size_t sx, sy;                  // local array strides (lx+2, (lx+2)*(ly+2))\n    size_t localSize;\n    int nbrLo[3], nbrHi[3];         // neighbors per dimension (0=x,1=y,2=z)\n    MPI_Datatype sendLo[3], sendHi[3], recvLo[3], recvHi[3];\n    MPI_Comm comm;\n\n    inline size_t lidx(size_t x, size_t y, size_t z) const noexcept { return z * sy + y * sx + x; }\n};\n\nstatic void blockRange(size_t n, int p, int c, size_t& off, size_t& len) {\n    const size_t base = n / p, rem = n % p;\n    off = c * base + std::min<size_t>(c, rem);\n    len = base + (static_cast<size_t>(c) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// Choose a process grid (px,py,pz) with px*py*pz == P that minimizes halo traffic.\n// Returns false if no factorization gives every rank at least one cell per dimension.\nstatic bool chooseDims(int P, size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, int dims[3]) {\n    double best = -1.0;\n    for (int px = 1; px <= P; ++px) {\n        if (P % px) continue;\n        for (int py = 1; py <= P / px; ++py) {\n            if ((P / px) % py) continue;\n            const int pz = P / px / py;\n            if ((size_t)px > nx || (size_t)py > ny || (size_t)pz > nz) continue;\n            // Per-rank exchanged face area; x-faces are strided, so weight them a bit more.\n            const double bx = (double)nx / px, by = (double)ny / py, bz = (double)nz / pz;\n            const double cost = (px > 1 ? 1.5 * 2.0 * by * bz : 0.0)\n                              + (py > 1 ? 2.0 * bx * bz : 0.0)\n                              + (pz > 1 ? 2.0 * bx * by : 0.0);\n            if (best < 0.0 || cost < best - 1e-9) {\n                best = cost;\n                dims[0] = px; dims[1] = py; dims[2] = pz;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return best >= 0.0;\n}\n\nstatic void setupDomain(Domain& d, MPI_Comm comm, const int dims[3]) {\n    int periods[3] = {0, 0, 0};\n    int mpiDims[3] = {dims[2], dims[1], dims[0]};  // MPI order: z, y, x\n    MPI_Cart_create(comm, 3, mpiDims, periods, 0, &d.comm);  // no reorder: rank 0 stays the output rank\n    int rank;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(d.comm, &rank);\n    int coords[3];\n    MPI_Cart_coords(d.comm, rank, 3, coords);\n    blockRange(d.nz, dims[2], coords[0], d.oz, d.lz);\n    blockRange(d.ny, dims[1], coords[1], d.oy, d.ly);\n    blockRange(d.nx, dims[0], coords[2], d.ox, d.lx);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 2, 1, &d.nbrLo[0], &d.nbrHi[0]);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 1, 1, &d.nbrLo[1], &d.nbrHi[1]);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 0, 1, &d.nbrLo[2], &d.nbrHi[2]);\n\n    d.sx = d.lx + 2;\n    d.sy = d.sx * (d.ly + 2);\n    d.localSize = d.sy * (d.lz + 2);\n\n    // Face subarray types, in MPI (z,y,x) order\n    const int sizes[3] = {(int)d.lz + 2, (int)d.ly + 2, (int)d.lx + 2};\n    const int l[3] = {(int)d.lx, (int)d.ly, (int)d.lz};\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        const int m = 2 - dim;\n        int sub[3] = {l[2], l[1], l[0]};\n        sub[m] = 1;\n        int st[3] = {1, 1, 1};\n        auto make = [&](int pos, MPI_Datatype& t) {\n            st[m] = pos;\n            MPI_Type_create_subarray(3, sizes, sub, st, MPI_ORDER_C, MPI_DOUBLE, &t);\n            MPI_Type_commit(&t);\n        };\n        make(1, d.sendLo[dim]);\n        make(l[dim], d.sendHi[dim]);\n        make(0, d.recvLo[dim]);\n        make(l[dim] + 1, d.recvHi[dim]);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void freeDomain(Domain& d) {\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.sendLo[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.sendHi[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.recvLo[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.recvHi[dim]);\n    }\n    MPI_Comm_free(&d.comm);\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const Domain& d) {\n    // Initialize owned cells and in-domain halo cells from the global formula\n    for (size_t lz = 0; lz < d.lz + 2; ++lz) {\n        const long gz = (long)d.oz + (long)lz - 1;\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= (long)d.nz) continue;\n        for (size_t ly = 0; ly < d.ly + 2; ++ly) {\n            const long gy = (long)d.oy + (long)ly - 1;\n            if (gy < 0 || gy >= (long)d.ny) continue;\n            for (size_t lx = 0; lx < d.lx + 2; ++lx) {\n                const long gx = (long)d.ox + (long)lx - 1;\n                if (gx < 0 || gx >= (long)d.nx) continue;\n                const size_t idx = idx3(gx, gy, gz, d.nx, d.ny);\n                grid[d.lidx(lx, ly, lz)] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nstruct Box { size_t x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1; };  // half-open local index ranges\n\n// 7-point stencil computation on a local box\nstatic void stencilBox(const Real* __restrict in, Real* __restrict out, const Domain& d, const Box& b) {\n    if (b.x0 >= b.x1 || b.y0 >= b.y1 || b.z0 >= b.z1) return;\n    const size_t sx = d.sx, sy = d.sy;\n    for (size_t z = b.z0; z < b.z1; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = b.y0; y < b.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t row = z * sy + y * sx;\n            const Real* __restrict c = in + row;\n            const Real* __restrict f = c - sx;\n            const Real* __restrict k = c + sx;\n            const Real* __restrict bo = c - sy;\n            const Real* __restrict t = c + sy;\n            Real* __restrict o = out + row;\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n            for (size_t x = b.x0; x < b.x1; ++x) {\n                // Simple averaging stencil (same operation order as the original)\n                o[x] = (c[x] + c[x - 1] + c[x + 1] + f[x] + k[x] + bo[x] + t[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Local index range [lo,hi) of globally interior cells within the owned block\nstatic void interiorRange(size_t n, size_t off, size_t len, size_t& lo, size_t& hi) {\n    const size_t g0 = std::max<size_t>(off, 1);\n    const size_t g1 = std::min<size_t>(off + len, n >= 1 ? n - 1 : 0);\n    if (g1 <= g0) { lo = hi = 1; return; }\n    lo = g0 - off + 1;\n    hi = g1 - off + 1;\n}\n\n// One stencil iteration with the halo exchange overlapped with the inner computation.\n// Global boundary cells are never written: both buffers start with identical boundary\n// values, which is equivalent to copying them every iteration.\nvoid stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output, const Domain& d, const Box& upd) {\n    MPI_Request reqs[12];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    Real* inp = input.data();\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Irecv(inp, 1, d.recvLo[dim], d.nbrLo[dim], 2 * dim, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Irecv(inp, 1, d.recvHi[dim], d.nbrHi[dim], 2 * dim + 1, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Isend(inp, 1, d.sendHi[dim], d.nbrHi[dim], 2 * dim, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(inp, 1, d.sendLo[dim], d.nbrLo[dim], 2 * dim + 1, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n\n    // Inner box: cells that do not depend on neighbor halos\n    Box in = upd;\n    if (d.nbrLo[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x0 = std::max<size_t>(in.x0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x1 = std::min<size_t>(in.x1, d.lx);\n    if (d.nbrLo[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y0 = std::max<size_t>(in.y0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y1 = std::min<size_t>(in.y1, d.ly);\n    if (d.nbrLo[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z0 = std::max<size_t>(in.z0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z1 = std::min<size_t>(in.z1, d.lz);\n    const bool innerValid = in.x0 < in.x1 && in.y0 < in.y1 && in.z0 < in.z1;\n\n    Real* out = output.data();\n    if (innerValid) stencilBox(inp, out, d, in);\n\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!innerValid) {\n        stencilBox(inp, out, d, upd);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Shell = upd \\ in\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, upd.z0, in.z0});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, in.z1, upd.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, in.y0, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, in.y1, upd.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, in.x0, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {in.x1, upd.x1, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n}\n\n// Gather the distributed grid into a global array on rank 0\nstatic void gatherGrid(const std::vector<Real>& local, const Domain& d, std::vector<Real>& global) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(d.comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(d.comm, &size);\n    std::vector<Real> packed(d.lx * d.ly * d.lz);\n    size_t p = 0;\n    for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.ly; ++y) {\n            const Real* src = local.data() + d.lidx(1, y, z);\n            std::copy(src, src + d.lx, packed.data() + p);\n            p += d.lx;\n        }\n    }\n    unsigned long long meta[6] = {d.ox, d.oy, d.oz, d.lx, d.ly, d.lz};\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> allMeta(rank == 0 ? 6 * size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(meta, 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, allMeta.data(), 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, d.comm);\n\n    if (rank != 0) {\n        MPI_Send(packed.data(), (int)packed.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 100, d.comm);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Receive blocks one at a time (avoids int overflow of Gatherv displacements on huge grids)\n    std::vector<Real> buf;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        const unsigned long long* m = &allMeta[6 * r];\n        const Real* src;\n        if (r == 0) {\n            src = packed.data();\n        } else {\n            buf.resize(m[3] * m[4] * m[5]);\n            MPI_Recv(buf.data(), (int)buf.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, r, 100, d.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            src = buf.data();\n        }\n        for (size_t z = 0; z < m[5]; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 0; y < m[4]; ++y) {\n                std::copy(src, src + m[3], global.data() + idx3(m[0], m[1] + y, m[2] + z, d.nx, d.ny));\n                src += m[3];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int worldRank, worldSize;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);\n    const bool root = (worldRank == 0);\n\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;              // global sizes\n    size_t lx, ly, lz;              // owned block sizes\n    size_t ox, oy, oz;              // global offset of owned block\n    size_t sx, sy;                  // local array strides (lx+2, (lx+2)*(ly+2))\n    size_t localSize;\n    int nbrLo[3], nbrHi[3];         // neighbors per dimension (0=x,1=y,2=z)\n    MPI_Datatype sendLo[3], sendHi[3], recvLo[3], recvHi[3];\n    MPI_Comm comm;\n\n    inline size_t lidx(size_t x, size_t y, size_t z) const noexcept { return z * sy + y * sx + x; }\n};\n\nstatic void blockRange(size_t n, int p, int c, size_t& off, size_t& len) {\n    const size_t base = n / p, rem = n % p;\n    off = c * base + std::min<size_t>(c, rem);\n    len = base + (static_cast<size_t>(c) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// Choose a process grid (px,py,pz) with px*py*pz == P that minimizes halo traffic.\n// Returns false if no factorization gives every rank at least one cell per dimension.\nstatic bool chooseDims(int P, size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, int dims[3]) {\n    double best = -1.0;\n    for (int px = 1; px <= P; ++px) {\n        if (P % px) continue;\n        for (int py = 1; py <= P / px; ++py) {\n            if ((P / px) % py) continue;\n            const int pz = P / px / py;\n            if ((size_t)px > nx || (size_t)py > ny || (size_t)pz > nz) continue;\n            // Per-rank exchanged face area; x-faces are strided, so weight them a bit more.\n            const double bx = (double)nx / px, by = (double)ny / py, bz = (double)nz / pz;\n            const double cost = (px > 1 ? 1.5 * 2.0 * by * bz : 0.0)\n                              + (py > 1 ? 2.0 * bx * bz : 0.0)\n                              + (pz > 1 ? 2.0 * bx * by : 0.0);\n            if (best < 0.0 || cost < best - 1e-9) {\n                best = cost;\n                dims[0] = px; dims[1] = py; dims[2] = pz;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return best >= 0.0;\n}\n\nstatic void setupDomain(Domain& d, MPI_Comm comm, const int dims[3]) {\n    int periods[3] = {0, 0, 0};\n    int mpiDims[3] = {dims[2], dims[1], dims[0]};  // MPI order: z, y, x\n    MPI_Cart_create(comm, 3, mpiDims, periods, 0, &d.comm);  // no reorder: rank 0 stays the output rank\n    int rank;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(d.comm, &rank);\n    int coords[3];\n    MPI_Cart_coords(d.comm, rank, 3, coords);\n    blockRange(d.nz, dims[2], coords[0], d.oz, d.lz);\n    blockRange(d.ny, dims[1], coords[1], d.oy, d.ly);\n    blockRange(d.nx, dims[0], coords[2], d.ox, d.lx);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 2, 1, &d.nbrLo[0], &d.nbrHi[0]);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 1, 1, &d.nbrLo[1], &d.nbrHi[1]);\n    MPI_Cart_shift(d.comm, 0, 1, &d.nbrLo[2], &d.nbrHi[2]);\n\n    d.sx = d.lx + 2;\n    d.sy = d.sx * (d.ly + 2);\n    d.localSize = d.sy * (d.lz + 2);\n\n    // Face subarray types, in MPI (z,y,x) order\n    const int sizes[3] = {(int)d.lz + 2, (int)d.ly + 2, (int)d.lx + 2};\n    const int l[3] = {(int)d.lx, (int)d.ly, (int)d.lz};\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        const int m = 2 - dim;\n        int sub[3] = {l[2], l[1], l[0]};\n        sub[m] = 1;\n        int st[3] = {1, 1, 1};\n        auto make = [&](int pos, MPI_Datatype& t) {\n            st[m] = pos;\n            MPI_Type_create_subarray(3, sizes, sub, st, MPI_ORDER_C, MPI_DOUBLE, &t);\n            MPI_Type_commit(&t);\n        };\n        make(1, d.sendLo[dim]);\n        make(l[dim], d.sendHi[dim]);\n        make(0, d.recvLo[dim]);\n        make(l[dim] + 1, d.recvHi[dim]);\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void freeDomain(Domain& d) {\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.sendLo[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.sendHi[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.recvLo[dim]);\n        MPI_Type_free(&d.recvHi[dim]);\n    }\n    MPI_Comm_free(&d.comm);\n}\n\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const Domain& d) {\n    // Initialize owned cells and in-domain halo cells from the global formula\n    for (size_t lz = 0; lz < d.lz + 2; ++lz) {\n        const long gz = (long)d.oz + (long)lz - 1;\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= (long)d.nz) continue;\n        for (size_t ly = 0; ly < d.ly + 2; ++ly) {\n            const long gy = (long)d.oy + (long)ly - 1;\n            if (gy < 0 || gy >= (long)d.ny) continue;\n            for (size_t lx = 0; lx < d.lx + 2; ++lx) {\n                const long gx = (long)d.ox + (long)lx - 1;\n                if (gx < 0 || gx >= (long)d.nx) continue;\n                const size_t idx = idx3(gx, gy, gz, d.nx, d.ny);\n                grid[d.lidx(lx, ly, lz)] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nstruct Box { size_t x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1; };  // half-open local index ranges\n\n// 7-point stencil computation on a local box\nstatic void stencilBox(const Real* __restrict in, Real* __restrict out, const Domain& d, const Box& b) {\n    if (b.x0 >= b.x1 || b.y0 >= b.y1 || b.z0 >= b.z1) return;\n    const size_t sx = d.sx, sy = d.sy;\n    for (size_t z = b.z0; z < b.z1; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = b.y0; y < b.y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t row = z * sy + y * sx;\n            const Real* __restrict c = in + row;\n            const Real* __restrict f = c - sx;\n            const Real* __restrict k = c + sx;\n            const Real* __restrict bo = c - sy;\n            const Real* __restrict t = c + sy;\n            Real* __restrict o = out + row;\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n            for (size_t x = b.x0; x < b.x1; ++x) {\n                // Simple averaging stencil (same operation order as the original)\n                o[x] = (c[x] + c[x - 1] + c[x + 1] + f[x] + k[x] + bo[x] + t[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Local index range [lo,hi) of globally interior cells within the owned block\nstatic void interiorRange(size_t n, size_t off, size_t len, size_t& lo, size_t& hi) {\n    const size_t g0 = std::max<size_t>(off, 1);\n    const size_t g1 = std::min<size_t>(off + len, n >= 1 ? n - 1 : 0);\n    if (g1 <= g0) { lo = hi = 1; return; }\n    lo = g0 - off + 1;\n    hi = g1 - off + 1;\n}\n\n// One stencil iteration with the halo exchange overlapped with the inner computation.\n// Global boundary cells are never written: both buffers start with identical boundary\n// values, which is equivalent to copying them every iteration.\nvoid stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output, const Domain& d, const Box& upd) {\n    MPI_Request reqs[12];\n    int nreq = 0;\n    Real* inp = input.data();\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Irecv(inp, 1, d.recvLo[dim], d.nbrLo[dim], 2 * dim, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Irecv(inp, 1, d.recvHi[dim], d.nbrHi[dim], 2 * dim + 1, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        MPI_Isend(inp, 1, d.sendHi[dim], d.nbrHi[dim], 2 * dim, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(inp, 1, d.sendLo[dim], d.nbrLo[dim], 2 * dim + 1, d.comm, &reqs[nreq++]);\n    }\n\n    // Inner box: cells that do not depend on neighbor halos\n    Box in = upd;\n    if (d.nbrLo[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x0 = std::max<size_t>(in.x0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[0] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.x1 = std::min<size_t>(in.x1, d.lx);\n    if (d.nbrLo[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y0 = std::max<size_t>(in.y0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[1] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.y1 = std::min<size_t>(in.y1, d.ly);\n    if (d.nbrLo[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z0 = std::max<size_t>(in.z0, 2);\n    if (d.nbrHi[2] != MPI_PROC_NULL) in.z1 = std::min<size_t>(in.z1, d.lz);\n    const bool innerValid = in.x0 < in.x1 && in.y0 < in.y1 && in.z0 < in.z1;\n\n    Real* out = output.data();\n    if (innerValid) stencilBox(inp, out, d, in);\n\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (!innerValid) {\n        stencilBox(inp, out, d, upd);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Shell = upd \\ in\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, upd.z0, in.z0});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, upd.y1, in.z1, upd.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, upd.y0, in.y0, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, upd.x1, in.y1, upd.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {upd.x0, in.x0, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n    stencilBox(inp, out, d, {in.x1, upd.x1, in.y0, in.y1, in.z0, in.z1});\n}\n\n// Gather the distributed grid into a global array on rank 0\nstatic void gatherGrid(const std::vector<Real>& local, const Domain& d, std::vector<Real>& global) {\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(d.comm, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(d.comm, &size);\n    std::vector<Real> packed(d.lx * d.ly * d.lz);\n    size_t p = 0;\n    for (size_t z = 1; z <= d.lz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 1; y <= d.ly; ++y) {\n            const Real* src = local.data() + d.lidx(1, y, z);\n            std::copy(src, src + d.lx, packed.data() + p);\n            p += d.lx;\n        }\n    }\n    unsigned long long meta[6] = {d.ox, d.oy, d.oz, d.lx, d.ly, d.lz};\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> allMeta(rank == 0 ? 6 * size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(meta, 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, allMeta.data(), 6, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, d.comm);\n\n    if (rank != 0) {\n        MPI_Send(packed.data(), (int)packed.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 100, d.comm);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Receive blocks one at a time (avoids int overflow of Gatherv displacements on huge grids)\n    std::vector<Real> buf;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        const unsigned long long* m = &allMeta[6 * r];\n        const Real* src;\n        if (r == 0) {\n            src = packed.data();\n        } else {\n            buf.resize(m[3] * m[4] * m[5]);\n            MPI_Recv(buf.data(), (int)buf.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, r, 100, d.comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            src = buf.data();\n        }\n        for (size_t z = 0; z < m[5]; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 0; y < m[4]; ++y) {\n                std::copy(src, src + m[3], global.data() + idx3(m[0], m[1] + y, m[2] + z, d.nx, d.ny));\n                src += m[3];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int worldRank, worldSize;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);\n    const bool root = (worldRank == 0);\n\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n                stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, d, upd);\n            } else {\n                stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, d, upd);\n            }\n        }\n\n        MPI_Barrier(d.comm);\n        auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n        auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n        if (root) {\n            printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n            // Calculate performance metrics\n            double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n            double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6; The executable is still called `stencil3d`, and MPI is always used.\n- **Splitting the grid:** the grid is cut into 3D blocks, one per rank. The program picks the block layout that sends the least boundary data between ranks, and avoids splitting along x where it can, because those faces are slower to send. If there are more ranks than the grid can be split into, the extra ranks sit idle instead of breaking the run.\n- **Boundary exchange:** each rank keeps a one-cell border copied from its neighbours, refreshed by non-blocking sends and receives each iteration. While that data is in transit, the rank computes the cells that don't need it, then finishes the outer layer once it arrives.\n- **Same results as the original:**\n  - The averaging uses the same operation order, including `/ 7.0`, which is why the results match bit for bit.\n  - Edge-of-grid cells are set once in both buffers and never written. Timing runs between two barriers, so it covers the whole computation. With `-r` or `-v`, the full grid is collected on rank 0 and the original checksum and validation code runs unchanged. It also matched on very small grids (3×2×4 and 9×5×4), with 0 iterations, and with up to 30 ranks so that some were idle.\n\n**Performance** (512³ grid, 20 iterations, one machine):\n\n| Run | Throughput (million cell updates/s) |\n|---|---|\n| Original serial | 459 |\n| MPI, 1 rank | 811 (the kernel is faster on its own) |\n| MPI, 8 ranks | 2,801 |\n| MPI, 32 ranks | 5,425 |\n| MPI, 64 ranks | 8,113 |\n| MPI, 128 ranks | 10,126 |\n| MPI, 256 ranks | 9,826 |\n\nSpeedup tails off above 64 ranks. That's probably the machine's memory bandwidth limit, since this stencil does little arithmetic per value it loads, rather than communication cost.
